## [2026-10-20 03:xx 夜轮] - RA-162（情况 A）：creator/discovery 双 importlib exec + 入口发现收敛
- 实现内容：新增 `lca/infrastructure/preset/plugin_loading.py`（`plugin_module_name` /
  `load_plugin_module` / `extract_entry` 三函数）；`loader.extract_plugin_factory`
  与 `discovery._load_plugin_tool` 都委托给共享 seam；`require_meta=True/False`
  把两线既有严格度收进显式参数（creator 线 PR12 `PluginMetaMissing` 不动，
  discovery 线 8-fallback 宽松启发式逐字保留）。模块名由绝对路径 sha256 派生
  （`lca_presets._exec.<name>.<digest>`），`_EXEC_CACHE` 按（模块名，内容摘要）
  复用：同路径同内容一进程只 exec 一次、两条管线拿到同一 module 对象；
  磁盘内容变化（热升级）则重 exec，保证永远加载最新源码。
- 改动文件：新增 `lca/infrastructure/preset/plugin_loading.py`、
  `tests/infrastructure/preset/test_plugin_loading.py`（4 测试）；改
  `lca/plugins/tools/cordis_control/loader.py`（`extract_plugin_factory` 委托，
  删 `importlib.util` / `PluginMetaMissing` 未用 import）、
  `lca/infrastructure/preset/discovery.py`（`_load_plugin_tool` 委托，删
  `importlib.util` / `sys` / `typing.Any` 未用 import）。`_load_plugin_tool`
  方法保留（scenario 测试 :390 的直接调用不动，最小 diff）。
- 测试：新 4 全绿（双管线同模块对象 + 旧双名永不出现 + exec_count==1 /
  PR12 缺 meta 抛 `PluginMetaMissing` / bare `execute` 回退可用 /
  热升级重 exec + 内容不变复用）；`tests/infrastructure/preset/` +
  `test_dynamic_tool_bridge.py` + ADR-0256 conformance +
  `test_cordis_creator_assistant_closed_loop.py` 共 28 全绿（含 scenario_5
  热升级+回滚）；`ruff check` 4 文件 clean。
- **Learnings for future iterations:**
  - **热升级重 exec 前先删 pyc（RA-162 实证）：** 见顶部 Codebase Patterns 新条目。
    同秒同尺寸改写是热升级/测试高频形状（v1→v2 只改几个字符），pyc 陷阱必中；
    本轮是先被自己的新测试抓到（`is not` 通过但 VERSION 仍是 1），再被 dbg2
    脚本（慢 import 把两次写分隔到不同秒）反证——“能复现→修→再验证”是标准动作。
  - **收敛双 exec 类 story，先跑热升级场景测试再定复用语义（RA-162 实证）：**
    第一版纯 `sys.modules` 复用直接干掉了 scenario_5 热升级（`total_dropoff`
    KeyError）。“同一路径只 exec 一次”与“热升级要重载”不矛盾：复用键必须是
    （路径，内容摘要），不是路径本身。先跑既有热升级测试，复用语义自然浮现。

`test_d1_shell_namespace_requires_approval`, `test_inv_06_shell_domain_triggers_approval`,

from the defer catalog via a real `ToolDeferSession` (shell namespace loaded) — their security
intents (shell → REQUIRE_APPROVAL, ADR-0256/ADR-0255 acceptance) are preserved, now via the
catalog instead of the hardcoded shadow. Criterion-2 net: the old S6 test now covers all 13
members through `build_default_approval_engine()`.

- `namespace_of_tool` is exact-match (`session._tool_to_namespace.get(name)`); the wire gate's
  "try twice" is the only reason the snake_case alias resolved — verified by reading

  `test_shell_namespace_triggers_approval` — the test always depended on the backstop, so the
  "stitch" necessarily re-sources those tests from the catalog.

## 2026-10-20 night — RA-168 landed (developer_message derive-path convergence)

Converged the three disagreeing derive paths on `surface/developer_message`
rows (cron handoff, ADR-0268 §6) onto one policy: **inclusion everywhere**.

- `lca_kernel/events/fold/inputs.py`: new `SURFACE_DEVELOPER_TYPE =
  "surface/developer_message"` closed-set literal added to
  `SURFACE_EVENT_TYPES` (kernel could not import `lca/session/surface_types.py`
  — layering — so the literal is explicit in the kernel with a docstring
  noting the DSH-extension vocabulary; unknown `surface/*` still fail-closed);
  exported from `lca_kernel/events/fold/fold.py`.
- `lca/runtime/session/run_session_writer.py::append_developer_message`:
  marker `"developer_message"` → `"append"` (RA-163 SurfaceOp-contract rule;
  the type-suffixed marker was the RA-163 "tool_result" mistake class).
- `lca/plugins/session/runtime/messages/messages.py::derive_event_message`:
  developer rows now project as `{"role": "developer", "content": ...}`
  instead of silent `None` — same shape the LLM wire emits.
- `derive_messages` docstring "retained differences" bullet updated (the
  RA-163 residual story is now landed, not deferred).

Files changed: 4 source + new test
`tests/integration/test_developer_message_convergence.py` (4 tests: kernel
eligibility + fold nodes, marker contract, wire/SSOT/transcript agreement,
job_id/run_id provenance).

**Learnings for future iterations:**
- **三叉派生路径收敛时，先确认 crash 路径是哪个（RA-168 实证）：**
  评估只确认了 fold 的 `_surface_op_of` 会抛；实现时发现真正的生产 crash
  路径是 `derive_messages`/`export_transcript`（它们对整本 journal 调
  `foldSurface`）——修 eligibility seam 本体，而不是在调用点绕。评估的
  "哪个版本更正确"要落实到"哪条调用链在生产里走"。
- **kernel 不能 import lca 时，闭集字面量显式写进 kernel（RA-168 实证）：**
  `surface/developer_message` 在 kernel 里是显式字面量常量（+ docstring
  说明 DSH 扩展词表），而不是前缀规则——闭集保持闭集，未知 surface/*
  继续 fail-closed，`test_known_types_fail_closed` 的 union 断言不受影响
  （union 去重，catalog 结果不变）。
- **marker 收敛和 type 收敛必须一次改完（RA-163 模式再现）：**
  只加 kernel eligibility 而不改 writer 的 marker，fold 照样 ValueError
  （"not surface-eligible" 变 "invalid surfaceOp"）——RA-163 的教训在这里
  又应验了一次。

suites 30+20 passed (3 pre-existing env skips); ruff clean on all 5 files.

## 2026-10-20 night — RA-169 landed (twin widget-fallback pipelines converged)

`lca/runtime/session/run_session_writer.py` 的两条 ~200 行孪生 widget 保底管线
（connector_auth / browser_preview：扫描 surface 事件找未挂载 tag → 向
assistant content 追加 `[widget:kind?...]`）收敛为一条参数化 seam：

- `_WidgetField`（wire 键/规范键/qs 别名/默认值/空值省略/fallback 键）+
  `_WidgetKind`（name/tag 正则/id 字段/字段表/fallback 来源/必填字段）两个
  frozen dataclass；`_extract_unmounted_widgets` /
  `_ensure_widgets_in_assistant_message` / `_parse_widget_qs` /
  `_fallback_widget_fields` / `_widget_field_value` 五个泛型函数；
  `_CONNECTOR_WIDGET_KIND` / `_BROWSER_WIDGET_KIND` 两个实例；
  四个公开函数（`extract_pending_intents_from_events` 等）保持原名/原签名，
  变为薄封装（`lca/runtime/session/__init__.py` 与测试的引用不受影响）。

附带发现并修复一个 pre-existing 红测试：
`test_browser_widget_deterministic_fallback` 的 fixture 用了 RA-163 之前的
dsh 风格 `"surface/tool_result"` 类型（c970b845d 起变红），按 RA-167 模式
把 fixture re-source 到 `SURFACE_TOOL_RESULT_TYPE`（不删 intent）。

Files changed: `lca/runtime/session/run_session_writer.py`（+178/-165），
`tests/contracts/browser/test_browser_integration_and_fallback.py`（fixture），
新测试 `tests/runtime/test_widget_fallback_seam.py`（4 个：第三种假想 kind
的 tag 提取/meta fallback/挂载跳过+去重/追加+omit_if_empty+不重复）。

**Learnings for future iterations:**
- **孪生管线收敛时，先把两份实现的"隐式默认"对齐成显式字段（RA-169 实证）：**
  connector 的 fallback 只读 snake_case 键、browser 读 snake|camel；ensure 的
  tag 参数一个省略空值一个全带——这些差异必须变成 `_WidgetField` 的显式字段
  （qs_aliases/fallback_keys/omit_if_empty），而不是在泛型函数里 if/else。
  字段表就是差异的清单。
- **stale fixture 的红测试先查 git 再动手（RA-169 实证）：**
  `git stash` 验证改前已红 + `git log -S` 定位到 RA-163 词表收敛，结论是
  fixture 过期不是产品 bug——修法是 re-source 到 SSOT 常量（RA-167 模式），
  不是放宽实现去迁就旧 fixture。
- **仓库不强制 `ruff format`（RA-169 实证）：** main 的同文件 `ruff format
  --check` 本来就不过；`ruff check` 干净即达标，不要顺手 reformat 制造
  diff 噪音。

Tests: widget 相关 21 passed（含新 seam 测试 4 + RA-168 的 4）; ruff check
clean on all touched files.

## 2026-10-20 night — RA-170 landed (delete the no-op reasoning-drop stage)

删除了 `lca/runtime/session/run_session_writer.py` 里永久 no-op 的
`_drop_reasoning_after_dropped_calls`（函数本体 + `derive_messages` 里的
唯一调用点）：它恒返回输入不变，而 journal 里根本不存在 reasoning-item
事件类型——这是为不存在的事件类型准备的假想 seam，不配占一个 pipeline
stage。原调用处留了一行注释说明删除理由。

orphan-drop 的 wire 语义（OpenAI `drop_orphan_function_calls` 镜像 +
PR-2 G-17 `orphan_dropped_count`）完全未动；删除在构造上就是行为中性的。

Files changed: `lca/runtime/session/run_session_writer.py`（-16/+4）。

**Learnings for future iterations:**
- **"保守起见留着"的 no-op stage 是技术债的温床（RA-170 实证）：**
  docstring 写着"No-op when..."的函数，如果那个 when 永远不成立，它就是
  死代码——删它比留着更诚实。deletion test 没过（删了什么都没浓缩）的
  反面：留着它是在暗示 pipeline 有这个 stage，新人会误以为 reasoning
  drop 是真实行为。

Tests: orphan-drop + writer + developer-convergence 16 passed; ruff clean.

Findings:
- **RA-171 (Strong, P1)**: "pending tool calls for the waiting_input
  checkpoint" is derived twice from the same `approval_request["tool_calls"]`
  shape with three silent drifts — `emit_approval_pause_from_result`
  (lifecycle_emit.py, fresh pause) vs `_pending_tools_calling`
  (recovery.py, restart recovery): fresh skips empty tool_name/call_id
  (recovery keeps ""), fresh injects `lca_run_id` into askUserQuestion args
  (recovery drops it — the frontend submit handler cannot resume a
  recovered run: silent failure on a production path), fresh records
  `step.tool_call.record`/pending_approval facts (recovery skips). No
  locality for the concept; two derivations = a real seam already hurting.
- **RA-172 (Worth exploring, P2)**: twin ~15-line TrustEnvelope bind rituals
  in `RunLifecycleCoordinator.execute()` / `resume()` (lifecycle.py:185–193
  vs 327–335) — ADR-0292 §10 P3 policy edits must land twice. Two = real
  seam, un-owned.
- **RA-173 (Worth exploring, P2)**: the resume bundle's ~24-field inventory
  is hand-mirrored between `write_resume_bundle` and `_restore_from_bundle`;
  only 4 keys are fail-closed in `validate_bundle` while the other ~20 get
  silent `""` defaults on read — the zombie session `validate_bundle`'s
  docstring claims to prevent but doesn't cover.

Not candidates (recorded with reasons): tool defer session is deep
(`new_defer_session` single derivation home, RA-160); reducer pause budget
is one seam one direction; `_assemble_runnable`'s inline assembler
construction is deliberate (recovery runs outside the composition root —
Speculative only); `task_cancelled` helpers and `apply_driver`/`RunTerminalizer`
aliases are compat hygiene (deletion test moves a one-liner inline); the
second `RunSessionWriter` in `resume()` is a same-method micro-dup.

Diversity quota satisfied (RA-171 leaky-seam/no-locality from the friction
walk — Strong; RA-172/173 the secondary duplication findings).
Not all-duplication.

Top recommendation: RA-171 first (only candidate with measurable silent
behavioral divergence on a production path; zero locality; drift already
three concrete differences). RA-172 and RA-173 are safe same-area follow-ups.

## 2026-10-20 RA-173 (worker session) - resume-bundle field inventory owned in one place
- **做了什么**：`recovery.py` 新增 `_BUNDLE_FIELDS`（frozen dataclass spec：name + required + session-attr extractor + bundle-read 恢复器），20 个 session 派生字段（trace_id/question/user_text/mode/plan_ref/agent_id/agent_name/device_id/plane/extra_plane/execution_target/assistant_id/user_id/topic_id/origin/developer_seed/developer_seed_job_id/attachment_ids/started_at/loaded_namespaces）全部收敛到该清单；`write_resume_bundle` 与 `_restore_from_bundle` 都走它，不再手工镜像。结构键（schema/run_id/approval_request/resume_point）仍是 validate_bundle 的 load-seam 契约，不在清单里。
- **校验扩展**：`question` 标 required，缺键/None 的 bundle 被 `validate_bundle` 拒绝（此前静默建成无 question 的僵尸 session）；其余 optional 字段保留原默认值并在 spec 注释里写明意图（mode→"solo"、origin→"user"、loaded_namespaces 缺键仍可加载即 RA-161）。写侧保持 best-effort：`question=None` 的 session 只会触发 `resume_bundle_invalid_skip` 警告跳过，不抛异常。
- **测试**：`test_run_restart_recovery.py` 16/16 全绿（含 2 个新增：`test_ra173_bundle_field_inventory_round_trip` 断言 20 字段 write→read 全覆盖 round-trip；`test_ra173_validate_bundle_rejects_missing_required_field` 断言缺/None question 被拒、删 optional 仍可加载）。`ruff check` + `ruff format --check` 干净。
- **Learnings**：
  - bundle 这类"写枚举/读重列"的手工镜像，收敛方向是 spec 三元组（extract/read/required）而不是简单的"抽一个公共 dict"——因为写侧要 session→JSON、读侧要 bundle→typed value、校验侧要 required 集合，三方消费形状不同，一个带默认值的 read 闭包比共享常量更贴合。
  - required 集合宁小勿大：只标 story 点名的 zombie 根因（question）；user_text/mode 等有合法空值或历史默认的字段标 required 会误伤老 bundle——"缺失即拒"只适用于"缺了就一定不是合法暂停态"的字段。
---

## Round — assess (2026-10-20 23:45, branch raphy/arch-20261020-2345)
- **范围**：YAGNI 取近 ~40 commits 热点。本轮唯一大热点是 canonical filesystem & storage（dc63652d1→17d3eeea2，15 commits：FileRecord schema、store 迁 workspace/user、SQLite index registry、FilesystemPromptSection、delivery gate、TrashManager、e2e 10 invariants），上一轮（07:45）评估过的 run lifecycle / resume / defer session / browser 区域本次跳过（RA-171/172/173 刚收敛，无新摩擦）。
- **摩擦走查**（端到端细读：file/store.py、file/trash.py、file/registry.py、file_record.py、delivery_gate.py、prompts/sections/filesystem.py、approval_engine.py、tool_defer/policy.py、0bb3878f1 diff）：
  - RA-174（Strong/P1）：`NamespaceApprovalStrategy.evaluate()`（approval_engine.py:224）在无 defer session 时手写 `{"shell": "require_approval"}` 字面量，而 SSOT 是 `DEFAULT_NAMESPACE_APPROVAL`（tool_defer/policy.py:46）——per-assistant override（0bb3878f1）之后，这是唯一绕过 override 感知接缝的路径；SSOT 加命名空间/改级别时该 fallback 会静默漂移。RA-167 同构（re-source 到 SSOT）。
  - RA-175（Strong/P2）：`LocalFileStore.put()` 硬编码 `origin="upload"`，但模块 docstring 称 store 由 gateway upload 和 write_file tool 共享；真实调用方 write_file（agent_created）、sandbox observation（agent_created）、computer exec（agent_created）的产物全部被错标为 upload，污染 registry 的 Provenance tracking。
  - RA-176（Worth exploring/P2）：FileStore Protocol 无 delete 接缝；TrashManager 走裸 rel_path + 自有 workspace_root + 自有 sidecar 格式绕过协议——trash 后 store.list()/read_bytes() 仍从 {attachment_id}/ mirror 读出该文件；id→meta.json+blob sidecar 手写了两遍（store 与 trash），two = real seam。注意：TrashManager 目前零生产调用方（仅测试），故事先钉接缝、不负责接线。
  - 评估后排除（记入 assessment.md）：_is_previewable vs classify_readability（回答不同问题：UI 直渲 vs agent 转换分级，PDF 即例）、store 的 /files/{id} 与 sandbox:// narrative 链接双寻址（互补设计：id 寻址在 user/ 时间戳改名后仍稳定）、validate_bundle 内联格式校验（RA-173 清单的 extract/read/required 三元组表达不了格式校验，收敛需第四成员，判 Speculative）。
- **运行时验证**：mock LLM agent.run smoke x2 → RUN1: completed | RUN2: completed（(a)(c) 通过；(b) 工具调用流由 01:45 轮 scripted-LLM 验证覆盖）；评估区域 e2e `tests/e2e/test_canonical_filesystem_invariants.py` 8/8 全绿。
- **Learnings**：
  - SSOT 旁的手写 fallback 是 fail-closed 语义下最危险的漂移点：DeferPolicy 的 default_factory 读 SSOT，而 approval engine 的无 session 分支手写字面量——两个"全局默认"一旦分家，静默 fail-open。以后任何"全局默认"只许存在于 SSOT，消费侧一律 `dict(SSOT)` 拷贝（可变模块级默认不得共享）。
  - provenance（origin）这类"调用方才知道的事实"绝不能由被调模块硬编码默认值冒充：store 的 `origin="upload"` 硬编码让 registry 的 Provenance tracking 从第一天就是错的。接口设计时问一句"这个值的知识在谁手里"，知识不在场的一方只给透传参数、不给默认值语义。
  - 新模块先有测试后有生产调用方是诚实状态（delivery gate / TrashManager），评估时不把"未接线"当架构缺陷写故事——但"接缝缺失"（protocol 无 delete）是真问题：接线迟早要来，接缝现在就该钉。
- Stories written: RA-174, RA-175, RA-176 (passes: false). Top: RA-174.

## 2026-10-21 00:23 CST - RA-176
- FileStore Protocol gains delete(attachment_id) soft-delete; LocalFileStore
  implements the full ritual: resolve registry record -> retire the live
  workspace copy into .trash/ (fall back to mirror blob) -> write the trash
  sidecar through the ONE shared write_sidecar_pair helper -> flag
  registry trashed_at. get()/read_bytes()/list() honor trash state via the
  registry (list() via get()); exists() stays physical (ingest dedup relies
  on it; AC only named list/get/read_bytes).
- Twin id->meta+blob pattern converged: write_sidecar_pair (JSON meta +
  raw blob) now serves both store.put()'s {attachment_id}/ mirror and the
  trash flat sidecars; trash_dir_for() shares the .trash/ location rule.
- Explicit mirror decision (assessment 要求): {attachment_id}/ mirror
  (meta.json + blob) is deliberately KEPT on delete -- stable
  /files/{attachment_id} address stays recoverable; reads go dark through
  the interface via the flag. Known follow-up: purge_expired cleans .trash/
  only, kept mirrors of purged files orphan (out of scope).
- TrashManager kept as path-addressed adapter (not deleted): trash_file
  resolves path->id via registry and delegates to store.delete(); no store
  -> fail-loud RuntimeError (the old standalone ritual was the bypass).
  restore_file/purge_expired unchanged (same sidecar format).
- FileStoreService gains delete passthrough (faithful FileStore).
- Files changed: lca/infrastructure/file/store.py,
  lca/infrastructure/file/trash.py,
  lca/infrastructure/capability/files/files.py,
  tests/infrastructure/file/test_trash_manager.py (rewritten through the seam),
  tests/e2e/test_canonical_filesystem_invariants.py (INV-FS-07/08 through seam),
  raphy/prd.json.

  - "delegates or deleted" 二选一时：看被删对象是否还承载独立 ritual
    （本轮 restore/purge 是独立的）——有独立价值就 delegate，无才删。
    AC 的 deletion test 只审判了 path-addressed trash_file ritual。
  - 反斜杠编辑走 stdin 时直接写 chr(92) 构造，绝不走 sed 穿 exec 的
    多层转义（本轮 sed 把 "\\\\" 吃成 "\"" 写坏一行，靠 ast.parse 发现）。

## RA-181 DONE 2026-10-21 — Converge the twin readability vocabularies onto one contracts seam
- Implemented exactly as assessed. `contracts/models/core/workspace/file_record.py` now owns the per-audience
  readability seam: frozen `ReadabilityProfile(tier, ui_preview)` + `readability_profile(filename, mime)`.
- Deleted `infrastructure/file/store.py::_is_previewable`; its LobeHub-aligned rules moved verbatim into contracts
  (`_UI_PREVIEW_MIMES` / `_UI_PREVIEW_EXTENSIONS`); `put()` wires both wire fields from one profile call.
- `read_text_preview`'s HTML/XML exclusion set moved into contracts as `READ_TEXT_PREVIEW_EXCLUDED_MIMES`.
- `bootstrap.py` artifact-closure `previewable` re-sourced onto the public seam; no private cross-seam import remains.
- Implementer decision (documented in module): NATIVE-but-not-previewable (.py/.sql/.yaml + unknown mime) is
  intentional — tier answers the agent audience, ui_preview the UI audience; convergence is at rule ownership.
  22-case old-vs-new equivalence matrix: 0 mismatches; wire shapes unchanged.
- Tests: 11/11 green (test_file_record_contract, test_file_store_and_write_tool, test_local_sandbox_output_mime);
  tests/scenario/file/ 7/7 green; ruff clean on touched files (UP042/UP017 in file_record.py pre-existing at HEAD).
- Learnings: 收敛"同一个问题两个词表"时先实证答案是否必须一致——本轮两个受众（agent 可读性 vs UI 内联渲染）
  的问题语义本就不同，硬合并会改行为；收敛形态取"规则所有权收敛、答案各表其受众"，分歧用文档化决策固定，
  再用 old-vs-new 矩阵实证零行为变更，这比机械合并表更安全。
